Test reports

Every GitHub message where someone says how far the game got on their machine. An AI read the comment and filled the fields (stage, platform, GPU, time); code checked that the GPU, commit and numbers appear in it, not that the stage is right. Nobody here reproduced these runs. Open the source before relying on a line. How this is counted.
